Method: FedDOSE框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 模块化引导的Tucker分解（Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition）块，用于编码高维dFC张量并高效捕获模块级时空模式；(2) 跨所有站点生成类特定原型，通过最优传输（OT）重心公式与Procrustes分析的组合在全球层面进行原型对齐，从而消除站点差异。

Result: 在三个多中心静息态fMRI数据集（ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II、ADHD-200）上对ASD和ADHD诊断进行大量实验，FedDOSE在ASD和ADHD检测方面均优于最先进的方法，展示了从多中心数据中学习鲁棒表征的有效性。

Conclusion: FedDOSE通过显式分解站点差异并利用动态功能连接信息，有效解决了联邦学习中统计异质性问题，为多中心fMRI数据协作分析提供了一种可靠且隐私保护的解决方案。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过最大化预测分布熵来校准大语言模型的训练方法，采用双层优化的框架调整损失超参数，并通过一阶近似使其在大规模LLM上可行，实验表明该方法在多项选择和生成式问答任务中均能实现良好校准，尤其在跨域泛化方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐通常使LLM过于自信且校准不佳。传统的后处理温度缩放本质上是域依赖的：在一个域上拟合的温度无法跨域泛化。因此需要修改模型参数进行训练，以从根源上改善校准问题。

Method: 提出以最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，直接抑制过度集中的预测以解决过自信问题。受温度缩放启发，将该目标实现为双层优化：下层在参数化损失下训练模型，上层选择损失超参数以最大化熵。为在LLM规模下实用，采用高效一阶近似，避免显式二阶计算。

Result: 在多项选择和开放式生成问答任务上，实验表明该方法能产生良好校准的LLM，尤其在跨域（out-of-domain）泛化方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 通过训练阶段引入熵最大化校准目标，可以有效提升LLM的校准质量，且相比传统后处理温度缩放更具跨域泛化能力，为偏好对齐后的模型校准提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），通过端到端的端点预测目标训练世界模型，避免了递归自回归展开带来的误差累积和梯度传播问题，在长时域预测任务上显著优于递归基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型大多使用局部几步预测目标训练，但在推理时递归展开自身的预测。这种训练与部署之间的不匹配导致：不同下游影响的转换在训练中被等同对待，局部小误差在递归推理中被放大，最终限制长时域预测准确度。

Method: DPWM采用非递归架构，将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入，并通过单次前向传播直接预测端点观测结果。该设计在预测和梯度传播中均避免递归展开，使长时域端到端训练在自回归训练不稳定的尺度上变得可行。

Result: 在连续控制和基于像素的基准上，DPWM相比递归世界模型基线显著提升了长时域端点预测精度，且预测时域越长优势越大。此外，使用相同长时域端点目标重新训练递归基线也能得到类似提升，验证了训练目标而非骨干网络是长时域预测精度的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 世界模型应在实际使用的时序尺度上进行训练和评估，研究焦点应从局部转换建模转向长时域预测精度。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [224] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）的内部安全机制，揭示其安全对齐具有稀疏性和跨架构可迁移性，并提出了一种全离线黑盒越狱框架SN-Guided Diffusion，利用加权安全神经元损失引导扩散过程，以极低的生成代价实现高迁移攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）作为一种替代自回归逐词预测的迭代并行去噪模型，其内部安全机制尚未被充分理解。研究者希望探究DLLMs在安全对齐方面的机制性脆弱性，从而为这类新兴模型的防御提供认知基础。

Method: 首先通过安全神经元映射和剪枝实验证明DLLMs的安全对齐是稀疏且可迁移的（从自回归前身模型继承机制性安全足迹）。在此基础上提出SN-Guided Diffusion框架：一种全离线黑盒越狱方法，通过加权安全神经元损失将扩散过程导向远离安全触发区域，实现良性/越狱提示的近乎完美分离。

Result: 自剪枝将ASR从2.6%提升至73.8%（LLaDA）和从1.9%提升至86.6%（Dream）；跨模型剪枝从Qwen2.5转移到Dream和Fast-dLLM分别达到73.2%和86.3%的ASR。SN-Guided Diffusion实现AUROC=1.0的提示可分性，迁移ASR分别达77.1%（Llama-3-8B）、86.9%（Qwen2.5-7B）和74.3%（Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite），仅需每个提示20个生成片段。

Conclusion: DLLMs的安全对齐机制存在根本性、可迁移的脆弱性，且当前越狱方法可以在全离线黑盒条件下以极低生成成本实现高成功率攻击，凸显了扩散架构在安全对齐方面的紧迫挑战。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Enforcing Opacity in Discrete Event Systems via Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06489)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散事件系统中带延迟观测下的不透明性（opacity）问题，提出了在该条件下的不透明性定义、验证方法，以及保证不透明性的传感器激活策略综合优化方法，并通过实例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人为引入观测延迟可以作为一种有效机制来掩盖系统本身，从而增强系统的不透明性和安全性。现有研究尚未系统解决延迟观测条件下的不透明性验证与综合问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先给出延迟观测下不透明性的形式化定义并设计对应的验证方法；随后将该分析工具扩展为综合工具，提出一种优化方法用于设计能保证延迟观测下不透明性的传感器激活策略。

Result: 提出了延迟观测下不透明性的定义与验证算法，以及基于优化方法构建的传感器激活策略综合方案，并用示例验证了分析与综合流程的有效性。

Conclusion: 人为引入观测延迟能有效提升系统不透明性，本文为其提供了系统性的验证与综合框架，为实际应用中的安全性增强提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Artificially introducing a delay in the observations of a system can be an effective mechanism to mask the system itself, with the goal to increase its opacity and thus its security. This work investigates opacity in discrete event systems with delayed observations. We focus on two questions: how to verify opacity under delayed observations, and how to synthesize sensor activation policies that guarantee opacity under such delayed conditions. To address these questions, we first introduce the definition of opacity under delayed observation and develop a corresponding verification method. We then extend such analysis tool into a synthesis tool by proposing an optimization approach for designing sensor activation policies guaranteeing opacity under delayed observations. An example is used to illustrate the analysis and synthesis procedures.

</details>


### [226] [Wasserstein Mahalanobis Distances for Recovering Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06560)
*Chuxiangbo Wang,Shiying Li,Caroline Moosmüller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein马氏距离，将经典协方差自适应度量从向量值数据推广到概率测度，用最优传输位移场替代欧氏位移向量，用Wasserstein切空间上的协方差算子替代局部协方差矩阵，并证明了其对非线性变换下潜在几何结构的恢复性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马氏距离是多元数据分析中基于协方差的自适应度量核心工具，能够恢复非线性观测下的潜在几何结构。然而经典马氏距离仅适用于向量值数据，无法直接处理概率测度（分布）形式的数据。作者希望将该原理推广到分布空间中，以支持直接在Wasserstein空间中进行的协方差自适应学习。

Method: 构造Wasserstein马氏距离：将欧氏位移向量替换为最优传输位移场，将局部协方差矩阵替换为定义在Wasserstein切空间上的协方差算子。通过理论分析证明该构造继承了ICA中的几何恢复性质，并针对共同协方差的高斯测度经光滑非线性推前变换的情形，给出与经典马氏距离之间精确及渐近误差界。

Result: Wasserstein马氏距离与变换后潜在均值之间的经典马氏距离近似一致；对仿射变换精确成立，对一般光滑变换在受控高阶误差项范围内成立。数值实验验证了理论预测，并展示了潜在几何结构的准确恢复。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了分布值空间中的经典马氏几何类比，为在Wasserstein空间中直接进行协方差自适应学习提供了理论基础，拓展了马氏距离在概率测度数据上的适用边界。

Abstract: The Mahalanobis distance is a fundamental covariance-adapted metric for multivariate data and plays a central role in recovering latent geometry from nonlinear observations. We extend this principle from vector-valued data to probability measures by introducing a Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance. Our construction replaces Euclidean displacement vectors with optimal transport displacement fields and local covariance matrices with covariance operators defined on Wasserstein tangent spaces.
  We show that this construction inherits the geometry-recovery property underlying nonlinear independent component analysis. In particular, for Gaussian measures with common covariance transformed by a smooth nonlinear pushforward, the proposed Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance approximates the classical Mahalanobis distance between the transformed latent means. The correspondence is exact for affine transformations and holds up to controlled higher-order error terms for general smooth transformations. These results establish a distribution-valued analog of classical Mahalanobis geometry and provide theoretical support for covariance-adapted learning directly in Wasserstein space. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate accurate recovery of latent geometric structure.

</details>


### [227] [Stationarity Floors and Vanishing Perturbations in Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06692)
*Samir Adly,Ba Khiet Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类确定性锐度感知最小化（SAM）方法在光滑非凸函数上的收敛性质。扰动半径为ρ‖∇f(x_k)‖^{1-α}（0≤α≤1），对于0<α≤1给出显式复杂度界（仅高于平稳水平(Lρ)^{1/α}），并由一维二次示例证明该界是紧的。对于α=0的未归一化情形需满足Lρ<1。进一步提出截断（clipped）规则，在远离平稳点时与常数ρ规则一致，在平稳点附近与梯度成比例，该规则保证‖∇f(x_k)‖→0及O(T^{-1/2})的平稳性界。数值实验验证了未截断规则的平稳性下限及截断的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锐度感知最小化（SAM）是一种通过扰动梯度来寻找平坦极小值的优化方法，但现有研究对其在常数半径扰动下的收敛性质（尤其是非凸情形下的精确复杂性刻画）认识不足。作者希望回答：恒定参数扰动规则在非凸光滑函数上究竟能达到何种平稳性精度？以及如何设计改进规则以消除该精度瓶颈。

Method: （1）分析扰动y_k=x_k+ρ∇f(x_k)/‖∇f(x_k)‖^α（0≤α≤1）的确定性SAM方法，推导其在L-光滑非凸函数上的复杂度上界；（2）构造一维二次函数反例证明该上界（平稳性水平(Lρ)^{1/α}）是紧的；（3）单独处理α=0的未归一化情形，给出Lρ<1的条件；（4）提出截断规则：在|∇f|较大时保持常数ρ扰动，在接近平稳点时扰动幅度与梯度成比例，并证明其恢复标准O(T^{-1/2})收敛率；（5）在二次函数、Rosenbrock函数和五维非凸函数上进行数值验证。

Result: 对于0<α≤1，得到了复杂度上界，其平稳性下限为(Lρ)^{1/α}，该界不能被改进（一维二次示例精确达到）。未归一化情形α=0需要Lρ<1。截断规则消除了常数ρ规则固有的平稳性下限，实现了‖∇f(x_k)‖→0和标准O(T^{-1/2})的平稳性收敛率，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 常数参数扰动规则在非凸光滑问题上存在固有的平稳性精度极限(Lρ)^{1/α}，不能通过理论分析改善；而截断规则通过自适应地缩小近平稳点处的扰动半径，有效突破了这一瓶颈，同时保留了SAM方法的性质并恢复标准收敛率。

Abstract: We study a deterministic family of sharpness-aware minimization methods for smooth nonconvex functions. The perturbation is $$
  y_k=x_k+ρ\,
  \frac{\nabla f(x_k)}{\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^α},
  \qquad 0\leqα\leq 1, $$ so that its effective radius is $ρ\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^{1-α}$. For $0<α\leq1$, we give an explicit complexity bound above the stationarity level $(Lρ)^{1/α}$. A one-dimensional quadratic example reaches this level exactly, showing that the bound describes a real limitation of the constant-parameter rule. The unnormalized case $α=0$ is treated separately and requires $Lρ<1$. We then introduce a clipped rule which agrees with the constant-$ρ$ rule away from stationary points and becomes proportional to the gradient near them. The clipped method has $\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}\to0$ and the usual $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound. Numerical tests on a quadratic function, the Rosenbrock function, and a five-dimensional nonconvex function illustrate the stationarity floor of the unclipped rule and the effect of clipping.

</details>


### [228] [The Blessing and Curse of Curvature in Higher-Order Optimization: Horospherical versus Geodesic Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06719)
*Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard流形上确定性强p阶（p≥2）预言机复杂度，针对强horospherical凸（h-凸）和强geodesic凸（g-凸）目标函数。在h-凸条件下获得欧氏最优收敛速率，可被负曲率进一步改进；而在g-凸条件下，曲率增长会带来信息论上的复杂度障碍，揭示了h-凸与g-凸之间的根本分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸优化在黎曼流形上的复杂度分析具有重要意义，但现有结果多集中于一阶方法或平坦空间。需要系统地理解p阶方法在Hadamard流形上的最优收敛速率，特别是曲率如何影响复杂度。h-凸与g-凸在欧氏空间一致但在弯曲流形上不同，这种分歧对优化复杂度的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者引入p阶条件参数Q_p=L_pR^{p-1}/μ，分别对强h-凸和强g-凸目标建立匹配的上下界。对h-凸情形，利用horoball支撑实现局部化到曲率尺度；对g-凸情形，在双曲空间构造硬函数族证明复杂度下界。

Result: 强h-凸目标在任意Hadamard流形上达到欧氏最优速率Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}，且负曲率可通过将Q_p替换为Q_p·min{1,4/(κR)}^{p-1}进一步加速。强g-凸目标在κR=O(1)时保持同指数，但在κR增长时存在硬函数族要求Ω~_p(Q_p^{1/p})次查询，与h-凸情形形成明显分离。

Conclusion: 这一结果明确了曲率在黎曼凸优化中的作用是双重的：它既能通过horoball局部化加速h-凸优化，又对完整g-凸类构成信息论障碍。h-凸与g-凸的分离揭示了在弯曲流形上选择合适凸性概念的重要性。

Abstract: We study deterministic Riemannian $p$-th-order oracle complexity (for $p\ge2$) on Hadamard manifolds, under strong horospherical ($h$)-convexity and strong geodesic ($g$)-convexity. The two notions agree in the Euclidean space. On a curved Hadamard manifold, $h$-convexity is a stronger notion than $g$-convexity and supplies global horospherical information.
  Writing the $p$-th-order condition parameter $Q_p=L_pR^{p-1}/μ$, we obtain the Euclidean-optimal rate $Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}$ for strongly $h$-convex objectives on every Hadamard manifold, with a matching fixed-curvature lower bound. On hyperbolic space, the resulting horoball supports enable localization to the curvature scale. This replaces the condition parameter $Q_p$ by $Q_p \min\{1, 4/(κR) \}^{p-1}$, subject to a logarithmic localization cost. Thus growing negative curvature ($κR \rightarrow \infty$) can further improve the optimal Euclidean rate under $h$-convexity. For strongly $g$-convex objectives, matching upper and lower bounds recover the same Euclidean exponent when $κR=O(1)$. In contrast, with growing $κR$, we construct a hard family on the hyperbolic space with $Q_p\asymp_p(1+κR)^p$ that requires $\widetildeΩ_p(Q_p^{1/p})$ queries. This reveals a fundamental separation: the same hyperbolic divergence that sharpens horoball localization yields an information-theoretic obstruction for the full $g$-convex class.

</details>


### [229] [Connecting Extreme-Point Generation and Decision Rules in Two-Stage Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06821)
*Jin Qi,Chen Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两阶段分布鲁棒优化问题，通过第二阶段对偶问题的极值点连接决策规则方法与分解生成方法，提出一种极值点生成算法来求解第一阶段问题，并开发了针对传统决策规则的后验精确性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段分布鲁棒优化中的决策规则方法需要预先指定自适应策略的形式，而分解生成方法则需迭代构造第二阶段价值信息。已有研究未能充分阐明这两种方法之间的联系，且缺乏一种高效且精确的求解框架。

Method: 通过第二阶段对偶问题的极值点来连接两类方法：每个极值点定义了一个仿射价值片段，并可通过相容的原基定义仿射策略片段。将极值点生成方法专门化以求解第一阶段问题，使用独立的线性规划程序不断添加片段直到在不确性集上恢复补偿价值，并基于算法输出开发后验精确性检验。同时给出了退化、秩亏补偿和结构化随机补偿情形的扩展。

Result: 所提算法在报告的实例上计算高效，结果显示在不确性集上完成第二阶段价值所需的极值点多于单独求解第一阶段问题所需的极值点数。

Conclusion: 该研究为两阶段分布鲁棒优化中决策规则方法与分解生成方法提供了统一的极值点分析视角，提出的高效算法不仅能求解第一阶段问题，还能完整恢复第二阶段的补偿价值与最优补偿策略。

Abstract: Two-stage distributionally robust optimization chooses a here-and-now decision and a wait-and-see decision policy that adapts to uncertainty realizations. Decision-rule methods specify the form of this adaptive policy in advance, whereas decomposition-generation methods construct second-stage value information iteratively. We connect these approaches through extreme points of the second-stage dual problem. Each extreme point defines an affine value piece, and a compatible primal basis can define an affine policy piece. Solving the first-stage problem may require only a subset of these pieces. We specialize an extreme-point generation method to solve the first-stage problem. A separate linear-programming procedure adds pieces until it recovers the recourse value over the uncertainty set, and the pieces can also be used to recover the optimal recourse policy. Building on the algorithm output, we develop a posteriori exactness test for conventional decision rules. We give extensions for degeneracy, rank-deficient recourse, and structured random recourse. The proposed algorithm is computationally efficient on the reported instances, and the results show that completing the second-stage value across the uncertainty set requires more extreme points than solving the first-stage problem alone.

</details>


### [230] [Preference-Based Estimation of Achievement Scalarising Function Parameters via Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06881)
*Maria Barbati,Salvatore Greco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序数回归的方法来估计多目标优化中成就标量化函数的参数（权重和参考点），并通过多目标背包问题的教学示例验证了该方法的可行性与解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成就标量化函数在偏好优化和多目标优化中广泛应用，但其有效性高度依赖于权重和参考点等参数的恰当设定，而手动指定这些参数是一项具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 提出一种基于序数回归（ordinal regression）的方法论，利用决策者（DM）提供的偏好信息来推断成就标量化函数的权重和参考点参数，实现从偏好信息到模型参数的自动转换。

Result: 通过一个多目标背包问题的教学示例逐步展示了偏好信息如何被转化为模型参数，结果表明序数回归为校准成就标量化函数提供了灵活且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: 序数回归能够有效支持成就标量化函数的参数校准和决策者偏好的显式化挖掘，为多目标优化中的偏好交互式方法提供了有前景的支撑工具。

Abstract: Estimating the parameters of an achievement scalarising function is a challenging task in multiobjective optimisation. Achievement scalarising functions are widely used in several contexts, including preference-based optimisation methods and algorithmic procedures for generating or selecting efficient solutions. However, their effectiveness strongly depends on the appropriate specification of their parameters, such as weights and reference points. In this paper, we propose an ordinal-regression-based methodology to estimate the parameters of an achievement scalarising function from preference information provided by the DM. In particular, the proposed approach allows us to infer both the weights associated with the objectives and the reference points characterising the scalarising function. The methodology is illustrated through a didactic example based on a multiobjective knapsack problem, which shows step by step how the preference information is translated into model parameters. The example illustrates that ordinal regression provides a flexible and interpretable framework for calibrating achievement scalarising functions and supporting the elicitation of decision-maker preferences in multiobjective optimisation.

</details>


### [231] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum: Analysis and Synthesis via Integral Quadratic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06915)
*Dennis Gramlich,Carsten W. Scherer,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用动态积分二次约束（IQCs）框架分析和综合加速随机梯度算法，将小批量梯度采样建模为Lur'e系统中的反馈非线性，提出一类扩展Zames-Falb乘子的IQCs来刻画小批量梯度的二阶统计特性，并给出认证指数收敛速率和渐近方差的半定规划条件，以及最优收敛速率的凸综合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度算法的收敛性分析通常依赖于强凸性和有界方差等假设，缺乏统一的鲁棒控制视角来刻画随机梯度噪声的统计特性，且现有方法难以同时分析收敛速率与渐近方差，也无法系统地综合出最优算法参数。

Method: 将小批量随机梯度建模为Lur'e系统中的反馈非线性，利用动态IQCs刻画mini-batch梯度的二阶统计特性；构建半定规划（SDP）来认证指数收敛速率并给出渐近方差的界；设计凸综合过程以寻找具有最小可认证收敛速率的小批量梯度算法。

Result: 提出了三类主要贡献：1）扩展经典Zames-Falb乘子的一族IQCs以刻画mini-batch梯度的二阶统计量；2）半定规划条件用于认证指数收敛速率和约束渐近方差；3）凸综合方法可识别具有最小可认证收敛速率的mini-batch梯度算法。

Conclusion: 动态IQCs为随机梯度算法的鲁棒分析提供了统一框架，不仅能够量化收敛速率与渐近方差，还能通过凸优化逆向综合出最优算法，丰富了随机梯度算法设计的方法论。

Abstract: This article applies dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) to the analysis and synthesis of accelerated stochastic gradient algorithms. We consider composite objective functions whose gradient can be approximated via mini-batch sampling and we model the resulting stochastic gradient oracle as a feedback nonlinearity in the spirit of Lur'e systems from robust control. Our first main contribution is a family of IQCs that characterize the second-order statistics of mini-batch gradients, extending the classical Zames--Falb multipliers. Our second contribution is a semidefinite-program for certifying exponential convergence rates of stochastic gradient algorithms, and a complementary condition for bounding the asymptotic variance caused by non-vanishing gradient noise. Our third contribution is a convex synthesis procedure that identifies mini-batch gradient algorithms with the smallest certifiable convergence rate.

</details>


### [232] [Stability of Differential Stochastic Variational Inequalities with History-Dependent Responses and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06923)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jian Guo,Xin Guo,Guan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一类微分随机变分不等式（DSVIs），将常微分方程与历史依赖的随机变分不等式耦合，用于建模具有时变随机均衡的闭环随机系统。在适当技术条件下建立了响应的唯一性、可测性和Lipschitz连续性，以及状态轨迹的存在唯一性；构造了基于独立样本路径的样本平均逼近（SAA）并证明其一致收敛；导出了参数变分不等式在移动可行集下的局部1/2-Hölder估计和轨迹稳定性界。在老年人健康监测系统中，通过相似性加权复用预计算响应，在将在线批处理时间从86秒降至不足1秒的同时，达到了接近全重计算基准0.97的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对历史依赖随机变分不等式与ODE耦合系统的系统性分析，特别是其可解性和数值逼近理论；同时，在不同随机环境间进行高效迁移学习缺乏定量保证，亟需理论框架来支持实际应用中的计算效率与精度平衡。

Method: 构建DSVI框架，将历史依赖SVI嵌入ODE动力学；通过技术条件证明二阶响应的唯一性、可测性与Lipschitz连续性，进而得到状态轨迹的存在唯一性。采用基于独立样本路径的SAA进行轨迹逼近并证明一致收敛；推导移动可行集下参数变分不等式的局部1/2-Hölder估计，并建立以初始状态差和Wasserstein距离为度量的轨迹稳定性界。实验采用相似性加权复用策略在老年人健康监测系统中进行数值验证。

Result: 理论方面：建立了DSVI系统的解的存在唯一性、可测性和Lipschitz连续性，SAA轨迹的一致收敛性，以及跨环境迁移的1/2-Hölder估计和量化轨迹稳定性界。应用方面：老年人健康监测系统中，相似性加权复用预计算响应达到接近0.97的精度（接近全重计算基准），在线批处理时间从86秒降至1秒以下；扰动和延迟更新实验表征了对传感器噪声的鲁棒性以及响应新鲜度、预测精度和计算成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为历史依赖DSVI系统提供了完整的理论和计算支撑，验证了其在高效迁移学习中的有效性，展示了在实时监测场景下通过响应复用策略在保持高精度的同时大幅降低计算成本的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a class of differential stochastic variational inequalities (DSVIs), in which an ordinary differential equation (ODE) is coupled with history-dependent stochastic variational inequalities (SVI). This framework models closed-loop stochastic systems with time-varying random equilibria and includes optimization-constrained ODEs as special cases. Under appropriate technical conditions, we establish uniqueness, measurability, and Lipschitz continuity with respect to the state of the second-stage response, and consequently the existence and uniqueness of the induced state trajectory.
  Moreover, we construct a sample average approximation (SAA) based on independent sample paths and prove uniform convergence of the approximate trajectories. For transfer between related stochastic environments, we derive a local $1/2$-Hölder estimate for parametric variational inequalities with moving feasible sets and a quantitative trajectory-stability bound in terms of the initial-state difference and the Wasserstein distance between exogenous path laws. Numerical experiments illustrate the SAA convergence and transfer-stability results. We further apply the framework to an elderly-health monitoring system. Similarity-weighted reuse of precomputed responses achieves an accuracy close to the full-recomputation benchmark of 0.97, while reducing the online batch runtime from 86 seconds to less than one second. Perturbation and delayed-update experiments additionally characterize robustness to sensor noise and the trade-off between response freshness, predictive accuracy, and computational cost. These results provide theoretical and computational support for efficient transfer learning in history-dependent DSVI systems.

</details>


### [233] [Certified High-Dimensional Wasserstein Robust Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07032)
*Chung-Han Hsieh,Rong Gan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化的可认证、可扩展的近似方法。通过将效用函数用支撑超平面进行上逼近并对支撑子问题对偶化，在one-norm度量下将原问题转化为多项式规模的线性规划，并提供了统一的理论误差界。实验验证了476资产月度调仓及1000资产的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化中，标准对偶得到的半无限凸规划在大规模资产数量下难以求解，而精确的顶点重构形式虽然精确但呈指数级规模，无法作为实际计算基准。因此需要一种既能提供确定性误差界、又能在高维场景下高效求解的近似方法。

Method: 首先给出订单一Wasserstein模糊集下的期望效用最大化问题的标准对偶形式（半无限凸规划）。对于long-only组合及box支撑和one-norm地面度量，给出精确的样本特定顶点重构（指数规模）作为基准。然后通过支撑超平面主化效用函数，并对支撑子问题进行对偶化，在箱式/多面体支撑上得到有限超平面-对偶公式；在one-norm地面度量及多面体组合约束下，该公式化简为多项式规模的线性规划。

Result: 理论方面，证明了均匀效用逼近误差同时界定了鲁棒值的误差和原鲁棒问题的近优性间隙。实验方面，验证了该认证近似的有效性，展示了476资产的月度调仓能力以及扩展到1000资产的计算可伸缩性。

Conclusion: 本文构建了高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化中首个兼具可认证误差界与多项式计算复杂度的求解框架，在理论保证与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模实际投资组合管理提供了可行的鲁棒优化工具。

Abstract: We develop a certified, scalable approximation for high-dimensional Wasserstein distributionally robust portfolio optimization. For expected-utility maximization under order-one Wasserstein ambiguity, standard duality yields a semi-infinite convex program. For long-only portfolios with box support under the one-norm ground metric, an exact sample-specific vertex reformulation provides an exponential-size computational benchmark. We then majorize the utility by supporting hyperplanes and dualize the support subproblems, obtaining a finite hyperplane--dual formulation over compact polyhedral supports. Under the one-norm ground metric and polyhedral portfolio constraints, this formulation is a polynomial-size linear program. The uniform utility-approximation error bounds both the robust-value error and the near-optimality gap for the original robust problem. Experiments validate the certified approximation and demonstrate monthly 476-asset rebalancing and computational scalability to 1,000 assets.

</details>


### [234] [Free-Horizon Newton Method for Nonlinear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07108)
*Gyeongrok Ha,Kenji Fujimoto,Ichiro Maruta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的轨迹优化方法，用于非线性l1最优控制问题，将控制时域作为自由变量处理，可同时评估控制持续时间和控制输入的l1范数，实现能量与时间效率的联合优化，并通过航天器Hohmann转移和地月转移仿真验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在固定控制时域下最小化控制输入的l1范数，无法同时优化控制持续时间与能量消耗，限制了轨迹的全局最优性。本文旨在通过将控制时域纳入设计变量，突破这一局限。

Method: 将控制输入和控制时域同时作为设计变量，扩展了基于牛顿法的算法，利用对这两个变量的梯度信息，实现能量与时间效率的联合优化。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证，该方法成功恢复了航天器Hohmann转移的解析解，并为地月转移确定了最优控制时域。

Conclusion: 该方法无需预先指定控制时域即可获得高保真的最优轨迹，在轨迹优化领域具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel trajectory optimization method for nonlinear l1-optimal control problems in which the control horizon is treated as a free variable, allowing both the control duration and the l1-norm of the control input to be evaluated. Unlike traditional approaches that minimize the l1-norm of the input under a fixed control horizon, the proposed method treats both the control input and control horizon as design variables, enabling joint optimization of energy and temporal efficiency. The method extends a Newton-based algorithm to accommodate this objective, leveraging gradient information with respect to both variables. This formulation enables one to find high-fidelity optimal trajectories without pre-specifying the control horizon. The effectiveness and robustness of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations, in which the proposed algorithm recovers the analytical solution to the spacecraft Hohmann transfer, and determines an optimal control horizon for an Earth-Moon transfer.

</details>


### [235] [Establishing Boundary KKT Convergence of Mirror Descent through Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07248)
*Kuangyu Ding,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明镜像下降算法在非凸问题下收敛到KKT点，无需排除边界极限情形。通过构造一个具有可定义边界延拓的"度量展平"重参数化S，结合KL论证和S的逆映射连续性，证明原始序列收敛到KKT点，并应用于Shannon熵、Fermi-Dirac熵和幂核等实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像下降算法在非凸问题中的收敛性分析一直是一个挑战，尤其是当迭代序列趋于可行域边界时，传统方法往往需要排除这种边界极限情形。作者旨在建立不需要此排除条件的收敛性结果，使理论分析更加完整和广泛适用。

Method: 核心方法是构造一个度量展平的重参数化映射S，该映射允许可定义的边界延拓。通过对重参数化后的目标函数应用KL（Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz）论证，证明S(x_k)收敛；再利用S^{-1}的连续性，恢复原始序列收敛到KKT点的结论。收敛性成立的充分条件联合约束了目标函数、Legendre核与可行域几何。

Result: 在可验证的联合条件下，镜像下降算法在非凸问题中收敛到KKT点，且不排除边界极限情形。算法被成功应用于Shannon熵、Fermi-Dirac熵和幂核等具体例子。

Conclusion: 该工作为镜像下降在非凸问题中的收敛性提供了更普适的理论保证，去除了边界情形的限制。未来可扩展到更一般的约束几何、非可分离核，以及更广泛的Bregman类方法（如Bregman近端点算法、Bregman ADMM及其非精确变体）。

Abstract: We prove that mirror descent converges to a KKT point for the nonconvex problem without excluding boundary limits. The result holds under verifiable conditions that jointly couple the objective, the Legendre kernel, and the feasible geometry. The key ingredient to establish the convergence is a metric-flattening reparameterization \(S\) that admits a definable boundary extension. Applying the KL argument to the reparameterized objective yields convergence of \(S(x_k)\). Continuity of \(S^{-1}\) then recovers convergence to the KKT point of the original sequence. We further apply our general framework to some concrete examples: Shannon entropy, Fermi--Dirac entropy, and power kernels. Future work may consider more general constraint geometries and genuinely nonseparable kernels, and extend mirror descent to broader Bregman-type methods, e.g. Bregman proximal point algorithms and Bregman ADMM, and their inexact variants.

</details>


### [236] [Inverse reinforcement learning for indefinite mean-field social optimization with multiplicative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07252)
*Ying Cao,Xun Li,Bing-Chang Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性二次均值场社会优化的逆向强化学习问题，针对带有乘性噪声和不定代价权重的系统，提出了基于模型和模型无关的两种逆向RL算法，分别证明其收敛性与稳定性，并通过数值仿真验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆向强化学习方法通常假设凸性代价和确定性系统，难以处理带有乘性噪声和不定代价权重的线性二次均值场社会优化问题，这些违反标准凸性假设的条件带来分析挑战，需要新的方法论。

Method: 提出两种算法：(1)基于模型的逆向RL算法，采用两个顺序循环分别处理个体和均值场动力学，求解耦合的随机代数Riccati方程和Lyapunov方程，并刻画恢复代价权重的非唯一性；(2)模型无关的逆向RL算法，利用积分RL和最小二乘辨识，仅需满足轻度秩条件的轨迹数据即可工作。

Result: 证明了基于模型算法的收敛性和闭环可镇定性，表征了恢复的代价权重的非唯一性，并通过数值仿真验证了两种方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般化的均值场社会优化逆向RL问题提供了系统性的求解框架，同时涵盖基于模型与模型无关两类场景，克服了非凸性和未知动力学带来的理论困难。

Abstract: This paper studies the inverse reinforcement learning (RL) problem for linear-quadratic mean-field (MF) social optimization. The considered system features multiplicative noise and indefinite cost weights, which violate standard convexity assumptions and pose analytical challenges. The goal is to recover unknown social cost weights from expert demonstrations and reproduce the optimal control policies. This requires solving coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations and Lyapunov equations with unknown system dynamics. To this end, we first propose a model-based inverse RL algorithm with two sequential loops that separately handle individual and MF dynamics, and we prove its convergence and closed-loop stabilizability. Moreover, we characterize the non-uniqueness of the recovered cost weights. To eliminate reliance on system dynamics, we develop a model-free inverse RL algorithm using integral RL and least-squares identification, which requires only measured trajectory data satisfying mild rank conditions. Finally, numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [237] [Metric Non-Collapse in Learned World Models for Control: Approximation Theory, Finite-Sample Geometric Guarantees, and Deterministic Planning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为非线性确定性控制系统的保度世界模型建立了一套三部分数学理论：近似理论（构造光滑精确潜变量实现并验证有限容量C^{1,1}逼近性质）、有限样本几何理论（提出编码器局部-全局度量合页损失防止折叠与崩塌）、以及确定性规划迁移理论（度量不崩塌与半共轭性保证轨迹与代价界及优化器迁移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习世界模型旨在从观测数据中构建可用于规划控制的潜空间动态模型，但现有方法缺乏对模型保度性（metric faithfulness）的严格理论保证，尤其是缺乏分离近似误差、统计误差与训练优化误差的有限样本分析，且对潜在空间不崩塌、不折叠的几何性态缺少可验证条件。

Method: 论文采用三部分方法：(1) 近似理论：对约束范数的张量积B样条类构造光滑精确潜变量实现，并证明容量无关的C^{1,1}正则预算下的有限容量逼近性质；(2) 有限样本几何：设计编码器仅的局部-全局度量合页损失，包含方向项（防无穷小崩塌）与分离对项（防全局折叠），在规则可观测因子、低Ahlfors覆盖与一致C^{1,1}预算假设下，给出有限样本偏差低于度量裕度阈值时每个近似经验极小化子的点态共Lipschitz性与近似受控半共轭估计；(3) 规划迁移：经由度量不崩塌诱导相容Lipschitz潜成本，半共轭性给出均匀轨迹与有限时域代价界及优化器迁移保证。

Result: 论文证明了L^2到L^\infty的指数在Lipschitz正则下是尖的；给出了可计算的单侧正则化阈值，超过该阈值时近似经验极小化子满足点态共Lipschitz性与均匀近似受控半共轭估计；数值实验采用有界潜范围、存档固定度量样本、有限容量系数盒样条代理与潜模型预测控制在受控摆上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该研究为保度学习的潜世界模型提供了从逼近、统计到规划迁移的完整理论闭环，将近似误差、统计误差与训练优化误差分离明示，为基于学习的模型预测控制的可靠部署奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a three-part mathematical theory for metrically faithful learned world models for nonlinear deterministic control systems. First, on the approximation-theoretic side, we construct smooth exact latent realizations and verify the required finite-capacity $C^{1,1}$ approximation property for norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes with capacity-independent regularity budgets. Second, on the finite-sample geometric side, we introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge whose directional and separated-pair terms prevent infinitesimal collapse and global folding. Although the prediction loss is observation-based, evaluation of this penalty uses state-metric supervision through observable-state distances and tangent directions. Under a regular observable-factor assumption, lower-Ahlfors coverage, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ budgets, provided the explicit finite-sample deviation lies below the metric-margin threshold, every approximate empirical minimizer above a computable one-sided regularization threshold is pointwise co-Lipschitz and satisfies a uniform approximate controlled-semiconjugacy estimate, with approximation, statistical, and training-optimization errors kept separate. The $L^2$-to-$L^\infty$ exponent used in this step is sharp under Lipschitz regularity. Third, for deterministic planning transfer, metric non-collapse induces compatible Lipschitz latent costs, while semiconjugacy gives uniform trajectory and finite-horizon cost bounds and an optimizer-transfer guarantee; learned latent cost heads enter through explicit compatibility errors. Numerical experiments use bounded latent ranges, archived fixed metric samples, a finite-capacity coefficient-box spline proxy motivated by the theorem, and latent model-predictive control on a controlled pendulum.

</details>


### [238] [On the Optimal Laplacian Jordan Structure for Synchronizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07286)
*Luca Dieci,Cinzia Elia,Alessandro Pugliese*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有预设图结构的微分方程网络中，如何选择网络的拉普拉斯算子以优化同步能力。作者在三角对角拉普拉斯算子类中给出了满足三个标准（同步解的渐近稳定性、拉普拉斯特征值归一化扩散最小化、最短瞬态过程）的充要条件，并将结果推广到带状拉普拉斯算子，最后通过大量数值实验验证理论并比较已有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络化动力系统中，同步性是一个核心问题。已有的研究关注如何通过设计网络拉普拉斯算子来获得最优的同步能力，但往往缺乏系统性的充要条件。作者旨在提供一个明确的选择准则，同时兼顾稳定性、特征值扩散和瞬态性能三个指标，以在给定图结构下找到最优的拉普拉斯算子配置。

Method: 论文针对具有预设图结构的微分方程网络，将问题限定在三角对角拉普拉斯算子类别中，通过主稳定函数（Master Stability Function）来刻画同步解的渐近稳定性条件。作者推导了满足三个优化目标（负的主稳定函数值、特征值归一化扩散最小化、最短瞬态）的必要且充分条件，并将这些结果推广至更一般的带状拉普拉斯算子情形。最后通过广泛的数值实验验证理论的正确性。

Result: 在三角对角拉普拉斯算子类中，作者给出了同时满足同步稳定性、最小特征值扩散和最短瞬态三个准则的充要条件，并将结果推广到了带状拉普拉斯算子。数值结果表明所提出的条件能够有效指导拉普拉斯算子的选择，并与现有工作进行了对比验证。

Conclusion: 论文为给定图结构的网络同步问题提供了系统的拉普拉斯算子选择方法，给出了严格的充要条件，并验证了其在三角对角和带状拉普拉斯算子类中的有效性，为该领域提供了理论支持和实用指导。

Abstract: In this work, for a network of differential equations with a prescribed graph structure, our goal is to show how to select the Laplacian of the network in order to obtain the most favorable outcome insofar as synchronizability. That is, we will want to: (i) guarantee asymptotic stability of a synchronous solution (as measured by a negative value of the master stability function), (ii) minimize the normalized spread of the Laplacian eigenvalues, and (iii) have a transient as short as possible. Within the class of tridiagonal Laplacians, we give both necessary and sufficient conditions for satisfying our three criteria above, and give extension to banded Laplacians as well. Finally, we give extensive numerical results to elucidate our theoretical results and to compare to existing works.

</details>


### [239] [Sampling and Optimization meet Enhanced Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07329)
*Yuan Gao,Siming He,Eitan Tadmor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两类具有增强耗散机制的输运-扩散动力学，使其快速收敛至Gibbs概率测度，并基于此设计高效的数值采样算法及相应的粒子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs概率测度在采样和优化中具有核心地位，其计算实现（即从$e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$采样）至关重要。现有方法收敛速度有限，需要设计具有更快收敛速率的动力学系统来加速采样过程。

Method: 作者引入两类输运-扩散动力学，其快速收敛的核心机制在于动力学本身所蕴含的增强耗散（enhanced dissipation）效应。基于这些增强动力学，进一步设计了针对目标Gibbs测度的数值采样算法，并给出了相应的粒子系统描述。

Result: 所提出的两类动力学能够快速收敛至目标Gibbs测度，并由此衍生出有效的数值采样算法和粒子系统方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用输运-扩散动力学中的增强耗散机制，可以显著加速对Gibbs测度的采样收敛过程，为高维采样和优化问题提供了新的理论框架与算法途径。

Abstract: It is well known that the computational realization of Gibbs probability measures, $e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$, plays a central role in sampling and optimization. In this paper, we introduce two types of dynamics that exhibit rapid convergence towards these Gibbs measures. The mechanism driving this rapid convergence is the enhanced dissipation associated with these transport-diffusion dynamics. Motivated by these enhanced dynamics, we design numerical algorithms for sampling from the target Gibbs measure. Finally, we provide the corresponding particle systems that may yield other effective numerical samplers.

</details>


### [240] [Solving Monotone Linear-Quadratic Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07336)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了N人广义纳什均衡问题（凸二次成本+共享仿射约束，仅要求伪梯度单调），证明求解变分广义纳什均衡（v-GNE）等价于求解一个由联合KKT条件导出的凸二次规划（QP），并通过正则化与加速优化方法实现O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义纳什均衡问题在工程、经济与博弈论中有广泛应用，但现有方法在仅假设伪梯度单调（而非严格单调）的情况下往往收敛慢或需较强假设。作者旨在建立v-GNE与单凸QP之间的等价关系，从而利用成熟的凸优化工具高效求解。

Method: (1) 将v-GNE问题等价转化为一个由玩家联合KKT条件推导出的凸QP；(2) 对该QP进行正则化，证明所得解为ε-近似v-GNE且次优性随正则化参数线性消失；(3) 提出加速近端点方法和加速投影梯度方法，两者在第k次迭代均达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE；(4) 在游戏雅可比可逆时，进一步将问题降维至较低维度的QP。

Result: 理论证明与数值实验均表明，所提加速方法在求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题上显著优于现有方法，收敛率达到O(1/k²)，且正则化方案提供了线性的ε-近似保证。

Conclusion: 通过将v-GNE等价为凸QP并引入正则化与加速优化技术，本文提供了一种高效、理论完备的求解框架，为大规模单调线性二次广义纳什均衡问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider generalized Nash equilibrium problems among $N$ players with convex quadratic costs and shared affine constraints, assuming only that the game's pseudogradient is merely monotone. We show that computing a variational generalized Nash equilibrium (v-GNE) is equivalent to solving a single convex quadratic program (QP) derived from the players' joint Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions. Building on this, we show that the regularization of such a QP yields an $\varepsilon$-approximated v-GNE with suboptimality vanishing linearly in the regularization parameter. Next, we propose an accelerated proximal-point scheme and an accelerated projected-gradient method, both attaining an $\mathcal O(1/k^2)$-approximated v-GNE at the $k$-th iteration. We also demonstrate that an invertible Jacobian of the game allows for reduction to a lower-dimensional QP. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments show the proposed methods substantially outperform the existing approaches to solve monotone linear-quadratic v-GNE problems.

</details>


### [241] [Uncovering expert objectives in production planning via inverse optimization: An industrial case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07398)
*Shivi Dixit,Rishabh Gupta,Adam Kelloway,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的逆向优化框架，从专家规划者的历史决策中推断隐含的目标函数。该方法将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划，采用次优损失逆向优化方法学习目标权重，并通过Dow公司的工业案例验证，显示了逆向优化可将隐性专家经验转化为可解释模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产规划中优化模型的目标函数难以定义，因为实际规划者需要平衡竞争目标、管理不确定性，并考虑难以量化的定性业务偏好。许多优化模型无法匹配专家行为，限制了信任和实际采用。

Method: 将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），未知目标函数表示为假设成本项的加权和；采用基于次优损失（suboptimality-loss）的逆向优化方法，从历史生产计划中学习目标权重；并扩展了时间和产品依赖的变体以提高预测精度。

Result: 在Dow公司真实工业案例中，推断出的权重表明避免库存短缺和维持一致的周期长度主导了规划者的决策。时间和产品依赖的扩展进一步提高了预测精度，并揭示了优先事项的演变。专家访谈确认了这些洞察的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 逆向优化能够将隐性专家经验转化为可解释的优化模型，为复杂工业系统构建更准确、更受信任的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Production planning in the manufacturing industry often relies on the use of optimization models, but defining an appropriate objective function can be a challenge. In practice, planners must balance competing goals, manage uncertainty, and account for qualitative business preferences that are difficult to quantify. As a result, many optimization models fail to match expert behavior, limiting trust and adoption. In this work, we propose a data-driven inverse optimization framework to infer the objective function implicitly captured in expert planners' decisions. We formulate the production planning problem as a mixed-integer linear program, where the unknown objective function is represented as a weighted sum of hypothesized cost terms. A suboptimality-loss-based inverse optimization method is then applied to learn the objective weights from historical production plans. The proposed approach is applied to a real industrial case provided by Dow, where the inferred weights reveal that avoiding inventory shortages and maintaining consistent cycle lengths dominate the planners' decision-making. Time- and product-dependent extensions further improve predictive accuracy and uncover evolving priorities. Expert interviews confirm the practical validity of these insights. Overall, this study shows that inverse optimization can transform tacit human expertise into interpretable models, enabling more accurate and trusted decision-support tools for complex industrial systems.

</details>


### [242] [A Domain-Specific Harness for End-to-End Automation of Optimization Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07407)
*Heechang Kim,Ernest K. Ryu,Shuvomoy Das Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoOPT，一个用于优化研究端到端自动化的领域特定框架，将最优一阶方法发现分为四个阶段：BnB-PEP数值设计、大语言模型符号发现与收敛证明、Lean 4形式化验证、人类解读与撰写。通过两个案例研究验证：新型加速梯度方法"lemniscate acceleration"在光滑凸函数梯度范数最小化上达到最优O(1/N⁴)速率，以及ITEM-f方法的解析描述（此前仅知数值结果），两个案例的收敛定理均在Lean 4中完成形式化机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化研究中的最优方法发现（如加速梯度法）通常依赖大量人工数学推导和验证，过程繁琐且易出错。随着大语言模型在数学推理上的能力增强，有必要构建一个自动化的端到端流水线，将数值设计、符号发现、形式化验证和人类解读整合起来，以加速优化理论的发现周期并保证正确性。

Method: AutoOPT框架包含四个阶段：(1) 数值设计：使用BnB-PEP（分支定界-性能估计问题）方法论进行数值搜索以找出最优算法结构；(2) 符号发现：利用前沿大语言模型从数值结果中发现解析描述和收敛证明；(3) 形式化验证：在Lean 4证明助手中对收敛定理进行机器检验；(4) 人类解读与撰写：由研究人员对结果进行解释并形成论文。

Result: 两个独立有意义的案例研究验证了框架有效性：(1) lemniscate acceleration——一种新的加速梯度法，用于最小化光滑凸函数的梯度范数，N步后平方梯度范数以最优O(1/N⁴)速率下降，常数为lemniscate常数ϖ；(2) ITEM-f的解析描述——此前仅以数值方式已知的方法，在L-光滑μ-强凸最小化中以每步(1-√(μ/L))²的加速线性速率收缩函数值间隙。两个案例的收敛定理均已在Lean 4中完成形式化机器验证。

Conclusion: AutoOPT成功展示了将数值优化设计、大语言模型符号推理和交互式定理证明相结合的全自动化研究流水线的可行性，不仅发现了新的加速算法（lemniscate acceleration）和解析化了已有的数值方法（ITEM-f），还通过Lean 4形式化验证保证了结果可靠性，为优化研究提供了可复现、可验证的自动化范式。

Abstract: We present AutoOPT, a domain-specific harness for end-to-end automation of optimization research. AutoOPT organizes the discovery of optimal first-order methods into four stages: numerical design through the BnB-PEP methodology; symbolic discovery of the analytic description and a convergence proof through frontier large language models (LLMs); formal verification in the Lean 4 proof assistant; and human interpretation and write-up. We demonstrate the framework on two case studies, each of independent interest. The first, lemniscate acceleration, is a new accelerated gradient method for minimizing the gradient norm of a smooth convex function: after $N$ gradient steps it reduces the squared gradient norm at the optimal $O(1/N^{4})$ rate, with a constant governed by the lemniscate constant $\varpi$, a classical elliptic-integral constant. The second is the analytic description of ITEM-f, a method previously known only numerically: for $L$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex minimization it contracts the function-value gap at an accelerated linear rate with a per-step factor $(1-\sqrt{μ/L})^{2}$. The convergence theorems of both case studies are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [243] [Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Linear-Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07433)
*Zhaoyu Zhu,Rui Gao,Shuang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则化的折扣线性二次型（LQ）控制问题中的Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）方法，证明了WPG在LQ设定下精确简化为有限维常微分方程（ODE），并证明了该ODE全局适定且从任意容许初始化指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein策略梯度通过在动作空间中进行传输来更新状态条件动作分布，但对其收敛性理论的理解尚不充分。作者希望在一个可解析处理的控制问题（折扣LQ控制）中严格刻画WPG的动力学行为，并揭示其收敛速率对熵正则化温度参数的依赖关系。

Method: 采用Bellman验证论证方法，证明无约束问题存在线性高斯最优策略；进一步证明折扣占用量加权的状态wise Wasserstein梯度正切于该策略类，从而将WPG精确约化为关于反馈增益和动作协方差的有限维ODE。随后对ODE的适定性和指数收敛性进行严格分析。

Result: 证明了该有限维ODE全局适定且从任意容许初始化指数收敛。对于每个固定的LQ问题，收敛指数在熵温度趋于零时存在正的极限，且不含形如$\exp(-c/\tau)$的微扰因子，同时保留了对控制问题条件数的通常依赖。

Conclusion: WPG在LQ设定下具有精确的有限维动力学描述，并且其收敛速率对熵正则化具有稳健性（不会因温度趋于零而出现小参数微扰导致的收敛退化），这一结果为正则化策略梯度方法的理论分析提供了坚实的解析基础。

Abstract: Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) updates state-conditional action laws by transport in the action space. We study entropy-regularized discounted linear-quadratic (LQ) control. A Bellman verification argument shows that the unrestricted problem has a linear-Gaussian optimal policy, and the discounted-occupancy-weighted statewise Wasserstein gradient is tangent to this policy class. WPG therefore reduces exactly to a finite-dimensional ODE for the feedback gain and action covariance. We prove that this ODE is globally well posed and converges exponentially from every admissible initialization. For each fixed LQ problem, the exponent has a positive limit as the entropy temperature tends to zero and contains no perturbative factor of the form $\exp(-c/τ)$, while retaining the usual dependence on the conditioning of the control problem.

</details>


### [244] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望成本（被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数之和。方法基于非光滑局部化下降引理，采用逐步细化的样本平均近似和Armijo型线搜索，在仅要求样本序列非递减且无界（无预设增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和驻点收敛性保证，并通过KL性质升级为全轨迹收敛及显式多项式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法通常要求正则项的（弱）凸性或随机预言机方差的均匀上界，限制了其适用范围。作者旨在解决更广泛的问题类别——包括光滑Lipschitz梯度损失与凸函数之差（DC函数）等结构，并放宽对样本序列增长速率的严格要求。

Method: 方法的核心要素包括：(1) 对期望成本使用逐步细化的样本平均近似；(2) 采用近端次梯度步长，通过Armijo型线搜索实现充分下降性质（容忍由采样近似引起的随机误差）；(3) 对目标函数中的被积函数提出一种非光滑、局部化的下降引理变体作为结构假设；(4) 利用KL性质将子序列收敛升级为全轨迹收敛，并推导收敛速率。

Result: 在样本序列仅需非递减且无界的条件下，证明了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛以及轨迹每个聚点的驻点性；该结果即使在光滑设置下也是新的。借助KL性质，获得了整条轨迹到单一驻点的收敛保证；对于指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量，导出了函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛率（至多差一个对数因子）。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架比现有方法适应更一般的问题表述，不要求正则项的（弱）凸性或随机预言机方差的均匀上界，并在收敛保证和收敛速率方面均提供了新的理论结果。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>
